Welcome back to the Draft Viewer! Today we've got a triple ***Magic** 2011* draft from the Top 8 of the Sealed M11 Block Champs.

Enjoy!

[![](https://media.magic.wizards.com/image_legacy_migration/images/magic/daily/activity/da523_ss.jpg)](https://web.archive.org/web/20100825000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=mtgo1513061)

## top 8 bracket

### Quaterfinals

**Boerge**

QuiGonJinn985

**vaarsuvius**

Shadow\_Prince

**mountainjam**

TPABHuK

**Duddits**

Triple\_Breaker

### Semi-finals

**Boerge, 2-0**

vaarsuvius, 2-0

**mountainjam, 2-0**

Duddits, 2-1

### Finals

**Boerge, 2-0**

mountainjam, 2-0

### Champion

**Boerge, 2-1**
